# The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation)

ספר מקבים א (תרגום כהנא)

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29)

אוסף יצירות מתקופת בית המקדש השני, שעמד על תילו כמה מאות שנים עד שנחרב בשנת 70 לספירה.

Ancient books written in biblical style that were not included in the canonization of the Tanakh.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Second Temple/Apocrypha

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): ספר המקבים (Segments: 923, License: unknown)

Version (En): Sefaria Community Translation (Segments: 14)


## Chapter 1

###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:1)


ויהי אחרי הַכּוׂת אלכסנדר בן פילפוס המקדוני אשר יצא מארץ כיתים [את חיל פרס] ויך את דריָוֶש מלך פרס ומדי וימלוך תחתיו בראשונה בארץ יון:


And after the defeat Alexander son of Philippus the Macedonian, who came out of the land of the Hittites [the Persian army] and defeated Darius the king of Persia and Media and reigned under him first in the land of Ione.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:2)


ויערוך מלחמות רבות וילכוד מבצרים וישחט מלכי ארץ:


And he will wage many wars and capture fortresses and slaughter the kings of the land.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:3)


ויבוא עד קצוֹת-הארץ וייקח שְלל-גויים רבים ותשקוט הארץ לפניו וירם ויגבה לבו:


And he will come to the ends of the earth and take the spoils of many nations, and the earth will be silent before him, and he will lift up his heart.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:4)


ויאסוף חיל כבד מאוד וימלוך על ארצות גוים ורוזנים ויהיו לו למס:


And he will gather a very heavy army and rule over the lands of the Gentiles and Counts, and they will have for him.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:5)


ויהיה אחר הדברים האלה ויפול למשכב וידע כי ימות:


And after these words he fell into bed and knew that he would die.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:6)


ויקרא לעבדיו הנכבדים אשר גדלו אתו מנעוריו ויחלק להם את מלכותו בעודנו חי:


And he called his honorable servants who grew up with him from his youth and distributed his kingdom to them while we were still alive.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:7)


וימלוך אלכסנדר שתים-עשרה שנה וימות:


And Alexander will reign twelve years and die.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:8)


וישלטו עבדיו איש במקומו:


And his servants ruled each in his place,


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:9)


וישימו כולם כתרי מלכות בראשיהם אחרי מותו ובניהם אחריהם שנים רבות וירבו רעות בארץ:


And they will all put royal crowns on their heads after his death and their sons after them for many years and increase evil in the land.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:10)


ויצא מהם שורש רשע אנטיוכוס אפיפנס בן אנטיוכוס המלך אשר היה בן תערובות ברומי וימלוך בשנת מאה ושלושים ושבע למלכות היוונים:


And from them came the root of evil, Antiochus Epiphanes, the son of Antiochus the king, who was a mixed race in Rome and reigned in the one hundred and thirty-seventh year of the kingdom of the Greeks.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:11)


בימים ההם יצאו מקרב ישראל בני בליעל וידיחו רבים לאמור נֵלכה ונכרתה ברית את הגויים אשר סביבותינו כי מן היום אשר סרנו מעמהם מצאונו רעות רבות:


In those days, the sons of Belial came out from among Israel and drove away many, saying, "We will go and make a covenant with the nations that are around us, because from the day that we separated from them, many evils have found us.


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:12)


וייטב הדבר בעיניהם:


And it is better in their eyes:


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:13)


ויתנדבו אנשים מן העם וילכו אל המלך וישליטם המלך לעשות כחוקי הגויים:


And people from the people volunteered and went to the king, and the king ordered them to do according to the laws of the Gentiles,


###### The Book of Maccabees I (Kahana Translation) 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20I%20%28Kahana%20Translation%29/r/1:14)


ויבנו גמנסיון בירושלים כמשפטי הגויים:


And they built a gymnasium in Jerusalem according to the laws of the Gentiles.
